About the Editors

Rajesh Kumar Dhanaraj, PhD

Associate Professor, School of Computing Science and Engineering, Galgotias University, Greater Noida, U.P, India

Rajesh Kumar Dhanaraj, PhD, is an Associate Professor in the School of Computing Science and Engineering at Galgotias University, Greater Noida, U.P., India. He has published over 35 articles and papers in various journals and conference proceedings and contributed chapters to books. His research and publication interests include cyber-physical systems, wireless sensor networks, and cloud computing. He is an expert advisory panel member of Texas Instruments Inc., USA. He holds a PhD degree in Information and Communication Engineering from Anna University Chennai, India.

Nilayam Kumar Kamila, PhD

Manager, Platform & Data Architecture Engineering Stream, Retail & Direct Technology, Capital One, USA

Nilayam Kumar Kamila, PhD, is an experienced Technical Architect and Lead, currently working as Manager in Platform and Data Architecture Engineering Stream under Retail and Direct Technology in Capital One, USA. He is currently an active member of IEEE and imparting his profound knowledge in the fields of science and engineering. He received prestigious professional certifications in machine learning, neural networks, and deep learning from Stanford University, USA. He completed his MS in Science, Master in Computer Application, and Master of Technology in Computer Science from Utkal University, India. He was awarded his PhD from Sri University, India, in 2017, in Computer and Information Systems.

Subhendu Kumar Pani, PhD

Professor, Krupajal Engineering College, Odisha, India

Subhendu Kumar Pani. PhD, received his PhD from Utkal University, Odisha, India, in the year 2013. He is working as a Professor at Krupajal Engineering College under BPUT, Odisha, India. He has more than 18 years of teaching and research experience. His research interests include data mining, big data analysis, web data analytics, fuzzy decision making, and computational intelligence. He is the recipient of five researcher awards. In addition to research, he has guided two PhD students and 31 MTech students. He has published 51 international journal papers (25 Scopusindexed). His professional activities include roles as book series editor (CRC Press, Apple Academic Press, Wiley-Scrivener), associate editor, editorial board member, and/or reviewer of various international journals.

His academic degrees and 12 years of experience working as a faculty in a global university like VIT University, Vellore, has made him more receptive and prominent in his domain. He has done more than 50 edited and authored books and collaborated with eminent professors across the world from top QS-ranked universities. Prof. Balamurugan Balusamy has served up to the position of Associate Professor in his stint of 12 years of experience with VIT University, Vellore.

Preface

Artificial intelligence (AI) is advancing at a breakneck pace, presenting unprecedented prospects to improve the performance of various industries and enterprises, including the transportation sector. Highly advanced computing approaches that imitate the way the human brain operates are among the AI advancements. The use of artificial intelligence in transportation aims to address the difficulties of rising travel demand, CO2 emissions, safety concerns, and environmental degradation. In this digital age, resolving these challenges in a more efficient and effective manner has become more conceivable due to the availability of a vast amount of quantitative and qualitative data, as well as AI.

Artificial neural networks (ANN), genetic algorithms (GAs), simulated annealing (SA), artificial immune system (AIS), ant colony optimizer (ACO), bee colony optimization (BCO), and fuzzy logic model are examples of AI approaches that are finding their way into the transportation area (FLM). A good grasp of the links between AI and data, on the one hand, and transportation system characteristics and factors, on the other, is required for successful AI implementation. Furthermore, it is hoped for transportation authorities to establish how to employ these technologies to quickly improve congestion relief, make travel time more predictable for their consumers, and improve the economics and efficiency of their critical assets.

Transportation experts and professionals are facing more complicated issues at the turn of the 21st century. They are challenged to provide safe, efficient, and dependable transportation while reducing the impact on the environment and communities. In recent years, transportation researchers and practitioners have become more interested in examining the feasibility of using AI paradigms to address some of the aforementioned issues in order to improve transportation systems’ efficiency, safety, and environmental compatibility. In the 1950s and 1960s, AI researchers frequently set grandiose goals for the subject, such as the development of generalpurpose problem solvers. Our goal in investigating AI applications for transportation as transportation researchers and professionals, on the other hand, is far more modest.

The book presents a comprehensive review of the difficulties and recent developments in the subject of smart mobility and transportation encompassing technical, algorithmic, and social elements. It examines innovative service and platform concepts for future mobility. Artificial intelligence principles are examined, as well as their implementation in modern hardware architecture. New machine learning (ML) issues for autonomous vehicles (AVs) and fleets are investigated in the framework of artificial intelligence. In addition, the book looks into the human dynamics and social implications of future mobility concepts. The purpose of this book is to present a comprehensive approach to smart transportation.

This book presents an overview of AI strategies used to solve transportation challenges around the world, with a focus on traffic management, traffic safety, public transportation, and urban mobility. Finally, the overview discusses the challenges and limitations of AI applications in transportation. The book examines modern technologies such as cloud computing, machine learning, and communication in the context of fully atomized transportation that meets residents’ requirements. This will result in a complete shift in transportation concepts. Highlighting most of the research directions in today’s security era, this book is more suitable for researchers from cyber security, machine learning analysis, data analysts, AI researchers, ethical hackers, students, and academicians.

Chapter 1 Introduction to Artificial Intelligence in Smart Transportation

GAURAV DHUURIYA, SHUBHANG SINHA, DEV AROA, TANUL PRABHAT RAI, PRASHANT JOHRI, and DEVANSH ARORA

School of Computing Science and Engineering, Galgotias University, Greater Noida, Uttar Pradesh, India

Abstract

Artificial intelligence (AI) has already impacted the ways of the world in which we live, and its impact is not slowing down but rather increasing at a drastic speed. It has created a world where the set of technologies are helping humans to solve problems very easily. AI has a major number of applications in several fields. One such field is transportation, where it has already created a major impact on how we move people and goods from one point to another. AI has helped us in transportation in far many ways from decreasing the number of accidents to traffic monitoring. AI has helped us to provide more safer, reliable, and efficient transportation. Not only that, AI has enabled us to provide more economical and sustainable transportation which not only has resulted in economic boom but also it has resulted with much cleaner environment. Its learning behavior has led to improvement in computation, monitoring but also in communication.

Its way of handling things and learning of new approaches has been exceptional and lead to many advances. Since its introduction, these last few decades have seen a boom in its growth and its aim to improve has been tenfold. AI leads to innovation and innovation leads to a better way of doing something, same can be said for transportation, whether it is tesla self-driving cars or AI-driven robots which may act as traffic enforcers in future and reduce the cost of labor, in this chapter we will look into details and some already existing applications of AI in the world of transport with respect to various changing factors and parameters. AI has strong set of subsets which can be used to further elaborate its power, one of these subsets is machine learning (ML) which is the art of programming a system by feeding it data and letting it work on its own. This can be implemented into the world of transportation as well. In layman’s term, the possibilities with AI are endless and we must use it to our advantage.

1.1 Introduction

Every day, the world we live-in is changing at a rapid pace, irrespective of the area or sector, there are heavy innovations which are making our work easier. AI is playing a huge part in this ever-changing world, whether it is sports, medical areas, or anything else AI is at the heart of its change. Transport is no different than these sectors, AI is capable of making it better than ever.

Artificial intelligence (AI) in transpiration has helped us to compete in many tasks such as self-driving car, directing person to his/her destination, provide most suitable path for the journey, it has led to reduction to less accident, less wastage of time, has prevented my accident, provided us forecast of weather, traffic, and majorly led to prevention of human error. AI has helped in damages caused due to human error which has us to increase at exponential rate. Now we don’t need to deal with unpredictability as almost every prediction can be made now with the help of AI.

AI has majorly impacted the security around the globe, which has resulted in much safer cities and locality. Transportation security has increased my folds and tracking of crime is much easier, which has boosted public opinion about public transports.

AI is already getting used in places with fixed-track transportation, such as railways and metros. AI is providing its assistance in this type of transportation in my ways, such as providing availability of seats, routes, and other features on the internet and even at devices inform of app and all. AI can also help in providing proper guidance to passenger about their destination and on notifying them on reaching there stop or station. It can lead to advancement where a train or metro is not getting man-handled and AI is driving these transports which would be more convenient as there will be less amount of human error. In future, we could loss imagine that these whole transport department is being handled by AI from providing ways to transporting people.

Air and Sea Transpiration has also been benefited by AI but not as much as Road Transpiration but still it has led to a major impact in this field. Such is the case of air transpiration where AI has helped in security as tracking of criminal from fleeing somewhere with the help of facial Recognition and other application of AI. It has also helped in tracking of illegal smuggling also. These are only few benefits mentioned but AI has led to uncountable benefits in these field of transport and it has a bright future in this field such as in case of water transport AI could help us in managing the navigation part which includes whole route travel and even help in transmitting of location without any human help and even maybe anchor the ship on its own. AI is craving at a path in air transport where whole flight will be piloted by ai alone or by single pilot as AI could pilot the plan at auto pilot now also.

Road transportation has been a place where AI has most strong hold and been in work since past two decades where the work on providing assistance in uncountable number of matters comprising from providing routes to monitoring traffic. The impact of AI on road transportation has been large, and it has changed the way people look towards the AI. There is various application of AI in this filed such as most basic one where they provide there assistance to driver where they guide them to their destination saving their time and effort as they provide the most reliable closest most efficient path for them preventing them to go through an unnecessary traffics. AI also helps authority to monitor vehicles so if they violate any rules, they could be dealt with. One of the most promising applications in this filed is driverless cars which had been highlights in this decade and they are most promising thing of AI in transportation, being able to travel without driving has been a dream, which is going to come to present. This could help in not only personal transportation but with public transport as well. Driverless vehicle is on its way to us and will be available for us in a short time.

There are many ways in which AI could be useful in field of transportation such as providing assistance and medical emergency to anyone who had been in accident as AI to alert authority in case of emergency. AI could also help in guiding people at a very easy pace so that they could cope up with it and thus decreasing waste of any human hours. It could even help in repair which will lead to prevention of losing any human life (Figure 1.1).

With AI in working transportation is more convenient, more reliable, more active, which has led to human to live more time to spare which was saved by them in transport. AI is a key to next generation of evolution in transport which could lead to more agile and intelligent transportation in the future.

1.2 Ai in Transportation

1.2.1 Global Market

AI has drastically affected the world’s economy and it is continuing to do so at the much faster pace. AI is going at a much faster pace and will be able to get hold of $1 trillion of the global revenue by the midst of the next decade. Last year, AI has increased its revenue from more a billion dollar to three dollars in the last two years alone while, it is predicted it will continue to grow like this and will reach a market share of $5 million by the end of 2024. The AI in transportation has been exponential and with its increased production it will be increase my fold in upcoming year. USA being the most influential among them all and increased sales by more than half of 100%.

1.2.2 Opportunities Created Through Ai Interventions

AI has uncountable number of applications in field of transportation one of them being autonomous vehicles (AVs) which has most influence in this field. Though other application is much more applicable in society, and they have a huge impact there, they are solving day-to-day struggle faced by human and they are overcoming them for the sake of humans and helping transportation to go much more easily and conveniently.

Through AI help, it is much easier to predict something more conveniently and it has become a more reliable source to work with. With help of application and internet AI can provide us best routes, paths to travel and cover our distance inform of Google Maps, Apple Map and many more. Thus, it has increased much satisfaction level among the users.

With help of AI-based application such as Uber, Ola, and Rapido a mode of transport can be at your home to take you to your destination just by one click. Which is more secure, economical and can help increase public transport globally [1]. There are many places where it is getting used one being USA’s economical hub NY where they have planned to lay smart bus track and make public transport more convenient and reliable for the people [15].

1.2.3 Efficiency

One of the most applicable applications of AI is security, road traffic deaths lead to almost 2 million deaths in a year. This was half in year 2011 and it doubled in a decade. Thus, we need application of AI to work to save people from these number of deaths which happens to poor infrastructure and human error and can lead to unnecessary deaths in the meantime due to not proper conveying of data regarding accidents but with help of AI we are overcoming these problems as AI application convey message to nearest authority is something unusual happens. Lastly self-driving car are being developed by tested and it will take over world with a storm and it is said that 1 in every 4 cars will be Autonomous Vehicle by the midst of next decade with a decrease in emission of gases globally [16].

AI can reduce the amount of emission at a very high scale by providing most suitable paths or routes, which led to fuel efficiency hence can led to less emission of toxic gases therefore preventing global warming [2].

There are eco-friendly AI trucks which are not only good for traffic management but are also helping our environment to stay green and healthy.

1.3 Artificial Intelligence (Ai) in Road Transportation

AI in road transportation is one field where AI is used at maximum level from guiding ways to monitoring of vehicles. There are an uncountable number of applications of AI in a single vehicle that are being used to help human to ease a little bit. AI in road transportation is at booming phase and everyone to explore this field, which has resulted in great economic generation. Automotive, technical, and investigations firms are trying to develop applications, sensors, command unit and software that make use of AI in that field. These has led to plenty amount of application of transportation in market increasing revenue and easing life of people [6].

There are many applications of AI in road transport one being automatic vehicles which are being used for commercial purposes. Not like autonomous motor vehicles these vehicles working in clutters where they compromise if group for trucks and named as truck platooning. In this, there are groups for trucks which are getting handled by AI and are maintain some distance for safe transport of goods. This is getting tested globally for increment of amount of transferrable goods at one time. These truck platooning could be handled by one single driver with the help of AI. In this, driver does not have to drive all the time but driving could be handled by AI alone and at sometimes driver need to indulge with it rest of the journey driver has just to monitor things for safekeeping (Figure 1.2).

There are also may benefits of AI in transportation such as booking of rides such as Ola, Uber, Rapido which has helped user not only to worry about boarding transport from somewhere rather than it has helped to providing services at their doors and wherever they want [15]. People now can share rides and even book their own personal rides with n number of possibilities of the type of vehicles they want. This has helped those ones who doesn’t have their own rides or the person who doesn’t have to take their rides at the location. It has also reduced the amount of emission that happened due to many cars leaving, due to sharing facility options in this application which result in less amount of car leaving [8].

One such application of AI in road transportation is autonomous motor vehicles or in short known as AMVs. AMVs takes the help of AI to for providing self-driving cars which doesn’t need any assistance from human but rather than they work on their own and carry passengers to their locations. AMVs not only can be applied in personal transport vehicles but also it could help public transport where it can run without any driver and carry numerous amounts of persons at the same time. Which will lead to less amount of human effort and can prevent unusual accident happened due to the fault of human driver and can reduce amount of emission and help in preventing Global Warming. Testing are being done of AMVs on public road and they are going with any stages of cases that they need to pass before they could travel on road by their own. One being AMVs in metropolitan cities where there is a large amount of traffic and roads are congested as compared to free-flowing highways. There is still testing that need to be done for monitoring AI behavior on these streets and they work on these congested streets.

Providing routs and ways to vehicles to reach their destination is one of the most used and underrated applications in Road Transport. They work on the principle of AI where they provide their assistance to human driver to reach their location at least amount of time by preventing traffic congestion and providing best route to travel on. Their aim is simple provide the best path by preventing traffic congestions which can be collected by them through internet where other AI devices sent information to. They compile data together and reroute the driver to ease them to reach their locations as quickly as possible.

AI can also help in Traffic Monitoring, by the help of machine learning (ML) internet of things (IoT) and many more. There are cameras set up on roads and highways at constant distance to monitor the flowing of traffic. In this data is collected by camera and send to clouds where AI works on Filtering things and track every movement of the vehicles. This could help in tracking vehicles, person through image recognition feature and help authority to reach to them without wasting any amount of time. They could even speed and movement of vehicles and even charge complaint directly and notify drivers about their faults so that there don’t repeat the same mistake again and again. It can also help in monitoring traffic through these and dispatch authority to clear the congestion at the right time. It could also monitor the people movements, and if any suspicious thing is happening, it can alert the authority which can take action accordingly. It could also alert medical authorities if any accident happens, AI is very useful in terms of providing security at any time.

AI can help in pre booking of Parking spots and even help in guiding vehicles driver to the parking without wasting time and even help foot passengers and bike driver to cross roads by stopping the traffic at any time.

There are many usefulness of AI in road transportation but there are many drawbacks and preventions that need to be take care of. One being violation of privacy, is your applications on car is hacked using AI het hacked it could lead to violation of privacy and even led to monitoring of your whole activity without even your consent and even without you knowing.

Security is one of the most drawback with AI in road transpiration if any public monitoring devices or authority database get hacked, they could manipulate anyone and even track anyone accordingly. This could lead to mass accidents and even violation of everyone’s rights. It could lead to manipulation of records and even lead to tampering with data in some cases and for removing some important data from the databases.

Loss of Jobs is one of the most concerned topics which has affected the human and which is going to do so in the process. If AI takes on this world there will be a time where there will be very less amount of human help needed. AI is taking over the work of human and at the process making them to go to other work thus creating as world where people are not sure about if they could hold their job anymore. This is creating disturbance in this world.

Lastly the topic which is in demand is it safe to give every right to AI for driving? Should we switch to Driverless Vehicles or Not? These are very concerning topic as if we give whole rights to AI and remove from any form of support who is going to take credit if any accidents happens and there is loss of life and goods. As AMV’s main aim is to let the driver relax and let AI handle everything and only taking help of passenger in case of Emergency [15]. What if the emergency arrives and there is no time to waste? This is a major concern as, if the driver is relaxing how could he take control of anything this fast and what if there is no time left to take any action. Scientists are debating on this, and they still don’t have reason to blame someone specific, who they should blame car owner or company owner who made that vehicles or AI who took that action. A few days ago one of the AMVs company owner stated that their AMVs are very safe and they are testing on streets with actual humans. Few days afterward their car couldn’t make right decisions and resulted in two deaths. These concerns can’t be left alone, and there needs to be some law regarding this before AMVs take on road.

Once there was a survey conducted to see who should be safe in case of a car accident, where you can either save car driver or 10-foot travelers. In that survey, 9 out of 10 told to save 10-foot passengers as there are 10 lives that can be prevented at cost of one. But what will AI do in this situation or any other situation like this? We don’t have answers to these as we don’t know what will AI do in this situation and who would it safe at this moment, and if something like this arrives who should take blame for this.

Though AI is very useful for road transportation we should not neglect that it could not make any unnecessary mistakes and that could be harmful to the society. There is concern that AI algorithms automate ethical decision-making autonomously or there should be any human intervention in this. AI is very useful in transportation till we have it under control and monitor it continually till it could make right decisions every time.

1.4 Artificial Intelligence (Ai) in Smart Metro Station

UAE’s authorities which are responsible for transport have encouraged a significant amount of usage of ML and AI with respect to the metro services in the country of Dubai. These new and advanced AI and ML will support crowd administration during summit hours at the majority of the stations, especially after a huge public event such as concerts and so on [19].

The intention of this project is to produce an interactive metro system with AI which satisfies the daily needs of the passengers with ease and efficiency. Furthermore, this project intents to transform the locomotive travel time for metro passengers by introducing certain patterns for the trains which will control the congestion in the schedule and other important factors. This sub-project needs an extend metro replica/model to control tours of metro trains throughout the entire days. It takes into account the tickets data, Algorithms of metro access and request, and a display screen and in-depth information about user’s journeys.

Outcomes of this scheme imply that metros cater to about 40% to 80% of passengers at particular or fixed locations during peak working hours and big public events/occasions, which is exhibited in the masses and more occurring runs [21].

The demo replica of this model revealed that user’s movement in the heavy busy hours will lead to intervals of congested traffic and more amount of waiting time for the users/passengers prior to boarding the metro. Fortunately, the extensive and smart use of AI and ML trimmed this blockage to 40–60%, and the standing duration to half an hour (Figure 1.3).

The AI technology added to offer particular time periods and reshaping the desire to enhance the convenience of the consumers by allowing the user’s specific boarding times, which enables them to reach their respective destinations on the required time. Although, these user benefits are under investigation at the moment alongside the confirmation of their usefulness and feasibility of undertaking the project shortly [21].

Big credit to the unified technological foundation, as now the RTA is more than capable of finding the intelligent explications to the objections with respect to its services, which include things like the enhanced exclusive demand. This body-always-prioritizes consumers and tirelessly works to satisfy their requirements for the sake of their happiness.

1.5 Artificial Intelligence (Ai) in Smart Rail Transport

AI and ML are intensely affecting both our secluded and professional lives, which is including our transport sector as well. Especially, the advanced and enhanced AI-driven projects and systems are hiking and strengthening the worldwide. Railway sector has been in need of huge improvements and reformations from the past 20 years or so.

Railway transportation is viewed easily after reformations with the AI because of its amiability, efficiency, and sum of money for compatibility in comparison to different ways of transportation. The responsible organization or group expects the advancement of the railways using AI and ML implemented at a high pace and time. There are some big challenges in the way of the rail industry to experience the positive effects of advancement using AI in full force, in order to overcome them, they need to extend their use of ML and AI in both the departments, i.e., railway developments and infrastructure [21]. While AI has been of concern over the past 60 or more years, only now it is starting to reach its full potential. As now we have access to advance level computing technologies which have the capability to gift us with high end ML/AI and superior UIs (user interfaces) which will ensure that these technologies are being used by as many people as possible and for their own benefits (Figure 1.4).

AI is enabling us to upgrade all transportation methods and make them more competent, more reliable, more intelligent, and safer. AI can help diminish traffic gridlock, recognize dangers, maintain transportation, investigate driving requirements, and even reduce arboretum gasoline effusions. Now, we are consulting about AI complimenting the railway industry to make improvements in things such as train time management and scheduling, managing the steam of a train, predicting collisions and preventing them, anticipating delays, enhancing management, and much more with an ever-increasing list of agendas. The AI advancements will improve the security of the public massively, passenger issues, and compress the all over the railway administration without compromising any of its primary functions. By implementing this method and protocol, the current system and model will work towards upgrading the trend for railway train industry to give up the share in stock markets, etc., in comparison to every other mode of transportation.

Even though there are possibly many advantages, AI also offers some complications and difficulties. AI utilization raises legal, budgetary, societal, and humane issues, for instance, what organization or person is accountable for any tragedy or loss of anything because of a cybercrime or attack or the case of integrity of information security or/and clarity. Moreover, this professes a possible menace to the masses and users, since the information can be used for monitoring which can lead to crimes indeed [12].

1.6 Automated Train Operations Systems

Although AI can be implemented for the upgradation of effectiveness and diminish expenses throughout the variation of rail(ways) administration, security, surveillance, and sub-division administration department, major illustrations of the implementation of AI in rail department and area is its enrichment of train functions/operations becoming automated [12].

Automation of train operation conveys accountability for being in charge of controls with respect to the operator of the rail command system, with non-constant and inconsistent levels/degrees of independence. The IEC (International Electrotechnical Commission) has found a four conventional levels of rail mechanization: the 3rd level correlates to fully automated functions/operations which does not require a driver and the 4th level to discreet (automated) train operations. The mentioned levels are already in practice in composed train systems and metropolitan haulage systems. The Al Maktoum International airport in Dubai functions as a completely exclusive rail for the transportation of their consumers from one terminal to other using a distinguished rail track model. This utilizes SelTrac, an automated train signaling technology that is programmed to command the rails automatically [12].

The SMRTL (Singapore Mass Rapid Transit Lines), the world’s biggest automated metro system, is a very good illustration of the railway transportation system which is at 4th-level mechanization.

In Europe, the prime significant idea about the initiation of Automation of train operation and AI assistance in railways is the placement of the ERTMS (European Rail Traffic Management System), which accommodates trains with an automated driver compensation technique.

Nowadays, superior AI technologies produce a strong unit to improve and help unbar the prospective of advancement of AI-driven railways. As intermodal transportation of cars, rails, etc., is anticipated to increase, models/projects are initiated to better program vessel movements on the rail network and improve real-time data-info interchange. AI might perform a crucial part in this.

1.7 Ai in Smart Traffic Control

The economy of a country affects directly by arrangements done to travel road in a better way. Network management and traffic monitoring are also the issues on which the government has to focus on. Signaling system is made improperly and traffic rules are avoided. It is about traffic intrusion leading to a blockage and malfunction in traffic management resources, due to this wrong person is accused for breaking the law. The gridlock of traffic at various intersections leading to global warming and pollution causes air pollution in cities, and due to high burning of fuel, it gets very challenging for the passengers who have to wait for long hours in cars. Today’s traffic management systems are unplanned and inclined to human error. The population is increasing day by day, daily passengers are increasing, there are condensed or narrow roads and global trade, due to all this, monitoring of traffic flow has become paramount importance in many countries.

Software with smart computers takes over the usual operating schemes after a technological explosion. Around the world, smart traffic management system provides improvements by providing ticket automation, strict traffic violations, safe public space, advanced solutions for traffic gridlock solutions in many major cities [13]. AI, controlled ML, computer vision, advanced IoT technology, and big data uses real-time solutions to traffic management problems. An intelligent traffic management system includes streetlights, active highways, high-resolution 3D viewing cameras, active roads and computer-assisted transportation display systems. By 2030, $25 billion is the expected reach of smart traffic management program with an 8.6% increase, according to Statistical reports. To provide better products, a lot of research is going on for smart traffic management.

The other objectives of the system are:

  • Creating a standard ticket with an electronic E-bill payment system;
  • Velocity sensors are used to warn passengers and drivers if speed limit increases;
  • By checking real-time traffic conditions, prioritize limited traffic conditions;
  • Accommodating the movement without congestion;
  • Eliminate pollution;
  • Big data and AI alter the performance of traffic monitoring systems and traffic control over existing synopsis;
  • Advanced monitoring systems at intersections and congested roads where appropriate GPS and GIS traffic direction is required;
  • Improving road communication by building an IoT, which has accelerated and improved intelligence systems;
  • Use a good lighting system that stores renewable energy sources;
  • Provide reliable public transportation timely;
  • The big data segment and AI strengthens intelligent traffic manage- ment.

1.7.1 Smart Traffic Control System

There are predefined time-based signaling method in many cities, but the shortcomings of such a method is that the traffic monitoring system works regardless of traffic flow. Therefore, the need arises for smart traffic control systems which would work according to real-time traffic conditions.

Components of smart traffic control system include smart signal lights, central control hub and AI-powered cameras and line systems. These components are defined below:

  1. Smart Signal Lights: Smart signals and traffic lights reduce traffic gridlock inefficiency and idle time on cloverleaves. It doesn’t matter what time schedule is defined earlier, smart lights can handle the chain and free up the traffic.

  2. Central Control Hub: Integration of central control system is done with signals, traffic radiation, chain detectors, and cameras. An AI-based system collects data from 3D AI-enabled mainframe cameras and queue viewing locations and thus, tests real-time data. For the free movement of traffic, this AI system helps to transmit an erudition clip, which is designed to control the operation of signals and traffic lights. The basis for traffic control system is the central control system (Figure 1.5).

  3. AI-Powered Cameras and Line Systems: Line system and cameras update information about real-time traffic status on the command system. The command system then helps overcome pollution and clear congestion by sending this real-time information [3].

1.7.2 Smart Traffic Monitoring System

Important components of intelligent traffic monitoring systems are IoT-powered intelligent communication systems and big data tools. The technical components that define this system are smart roads, intelligent parking, traffic lamps, geospatial traffic management system and public transport. These systems change the normal functions of tickets through automation by providing superior assistance through the IoT-enabled information. Integrated sensor services, automotive information systems, and private vehicle tracking devices for tracking location. Data is accessed through an app related to parking spaces at a specific location and this data is enabled by Smart parking for better assistance. Smart highways and roads use AI technology in an increased manner [3]. To monitor the vehicle’s speed and inform car owners of the proposed information systems for vehicles on the speed limit, information regarding long distances and other categories of information, roads, and highways are equipped with sensors. Geospatial traffic control system is a combination of radiofrequency devices, GPS, and GIS. Based on physical object’s location, it translates 3D rendering of real-time geographical data. GPS is currently used to find the best routes to reach specific goals in a short time based on traffic in all possible directions. By providing anticipated potential obstacles, surveillance, and traffic congestion from a variety of routes and their specific queue lengths, geospatial technology can enhance the system.

1.7.3 Traffic Management Solutions

Traffic control lights are the only thing which is dealt with by historical traffic management systems. Algorithmic solutions are very limited in solving this problem and expert system (ES) solutions are also not the best, as they have shown valid results only for signal networks which are small. As a result, the need for management of interstate highways, emergency response, overpass system and public transport is left ignored. For the advance management of traffic, a distributed blackboard system has been developed by Georgia Tech to put to use in large urban areas. To solve vehicle control issues of individuals that appear on the data structure of blackboard various things are used by sources like frame, neural network presentations, law, and script [4]. There are problems in control, monitoring, and congestion of traffic, event management and dynamic communication which are also dealt with by the sources in the system. Then the formation of traffic management decisions is done which are evaluated by simulation later on.

Certain ATMS activities includes the following:

  • Display of the above streetlights is linked;
  • Metering of ramp on congested highways;
  • Traffic flow modeling is predicted;
  • Monitoring of traffic is done;
  • The enforcement of electric regulations of traffic;
  • Algorithms to detect incidents;
  • Opening of reversible and reversed routes;
  • Control and monitoring of road;
  • Needs management;
  • A collection of automatic toll payments; • Application for emergency/police vehicles.

Advanced traffic management system (ATMS) responds to dynamic traffic conditions by effectively managing available traffic resources. All modes of transport must be included in the advanced vehicle control system (VCS) in order to provide management solution like taxis, trucks, and various other vehicles. Advanced traveler information system (ATIS) provides the traveler with access to options, information special events for any given area. The accommodation details and services will also be provided to travelers. Therefore, ATIS is considered as the support system for ATMS’s construction. Traffic congestion reports, police or community safety reports, vehicle management decisions, model predictions, ship line schedules and status, truck trading, event schedules, attendees, travel, and status schedules, airport schedules and status, emergency calls, incident reports, road spots, trade traffic reports, weather reports, street sensors, video cameras, cell phones, places of interest, accommodation guide, service directory are the inputs which are included in ATIS. ATIS also provides information to travelers on the existence and availability of services, car accident warning and road sign, the best routes you can take, the best travel available, availability of parking and predicted dense areas during the day [5].

Control decisions are applied on all traffic aspects, using the database of ATIS, to manage traffic by ATMs. Environmental aspects are addressed, if any, like traffic pollution, and traffic is made easier by the decisions made. Traffic control, incident management, and driver information systems are the basic elements which are used by ATM to maintain smooth traffic. There are many methods used by ATMS to manage traffic. They include:

  • Reversible routes;
  • Signal lights;
  • Hazard warning to vehicles;
  • Flexible road signs;
  • Traffic signings;
  • Measuring meters; and
  • Emergency vehicle signals.

To maintain the traffic flow, these subsystems must work in concert. The traffic control system is useless if it cannot alert drivers to avoid a problem area and respond to accidents [23]. Many countries are still working on to implement the basics of management systems, while many countries have already done so. This shows the deployment phase of ATMS. Many modern systems have flexible control capabilities, traffic control algorithms are not just a simple timeline for the day-to-day signal systems, they are more than that. To assess the stop times, to reduce the average queues, and to determine the negative effects on traffic, control algorithms are used, namely, SCOOT and TRANSYT. United Kingdom and China uses SCOOT system currently. However, the initial system parameter is required to be adjusted for SCOOT system to ensure that its models conform to traffic visibility. To ensure system performance, periodic updates are also required. National Cheng-Kung University in Taiwan has developed computerized systems to control dynamic traffic.

There are some existing street patterns and preset tables. Based on that COMDYCS-I sets the signal time schedule in its first program. While stepwise adjustment of signal timing is done as well as pre-defined traffic models are used, to determine the signal time programs logically, by COMDYCS-III. Los Angeles Automated Traffic Surveillance and Control system is a control system which is much responsive and it is used to control signal light. Signal time system is selected by comparing actual data to available data. The future plans of ATSAC’s are the development of a program that is specialized to assist in identifying non-recurring congestion and also provides assistance to reduce the traffic for various categories of non-recurring events [23]. The Sydney Coordinated Adaptive Traffic System which is developed in Australia but distributed in Hong Kong and Singapore is one of the most developed systems in the world. Realtime traffic data is collected from local computers and then transmitted to a central regional computer. This is done by SCATS system. Then the central computer selects the signal timing plan based on the information which was collected earlier.

The parameters within these systems are optimized, however, by the central computer depending on the real-time response at the local level to optimize traffic flow. TERMINUS uses the neural model to expand the philosophy of systems to create real-time traffic flow data and to determine signal light setting that will make the flow highly efficient. It uses real-time data which is collected at the local level to determine the signal light configuration, just like SCATS do. However, in TERMINUS, different configurations are determined for the current state in real time. Thus, greater flexibility is achieved in the system because different situations that occur in everyday traffic cannot be tracked by very complex systems. Detection and notification are the main functions of an incident management system. Motorists or law enforcement agencies often report traffic incidents. Response steps and ATM detection are automatic. More systems are being tested and upgraded, however. On-board automotive information systems include Japan’s Road Automobile Communication Systems and Advanced Mobile Traffic Information and Communication Systems. To show other routes and problems to drivers on in-car monitors, RACS and AMTICS use microwaves and cellular connectors to local traffic stations and also digital CD-ROM maps. Similarly, to assist motorists, network connectivity is used by European DRIVE.

In this method of implementation, the AI and ML-inspired platform allows vehicular assets to plan, establish, and control the management of services using any class of vehicular asset. This allows fluidity among the providers to provide the deliverables that transporters require, such as hailing rides, robo-taxi, micro-transit and shuttles which are independent – every one of these helps the masses to travel from position D to E, in possibly in the utmost efficient method or way [18]. This method can also be implemented to deal with festival traffic management but it might require patience and research budget with a little cooperation from the governing bodies.

Alongside the implementation of AI and ML to enhance the program of festive control in order for it continually grow/improve its ability/ capacity and efficiency, to shape this program into something which is revolutionary in the transport industry. 1.9 Uses of Artificial Intelligence (Ai) in Natural Disaster Traffic Control

All over the planet, natural calamities have been a regular and normal occurrence. These disasters have been increasing over the years for a lot of reasons. In the year 2017, typhoons, quakes, and forest fires caused a loss of $306 billion globally, which is around 2-fold than 2016’s loss which was $188 billion.

These calamities are a consequence of weather variation, violent weather, aging inadequately shaped foundation, amongst various other problems, there is a problem which relates to the well-being of us Humans and our lives, due to natural disasters. Universally, 94 trillion dollars investment is required to maintain the speed which corresponds community progress as well, with a huge part of it invested in the restoration of our natural habitat. That being said, a large amount must be invested in the well-being and traffic organization during disasters so loss of human life and vehicles can be reduced as every year in traffic accidents caused by natural disasters is a lot and we can use AI for this purpose.

One of the simple explanations can be understood with the help of this given diagram, here it implies that we can predict the state of the traffic and manage the traffic accordingly; similar to festive traffic management, using deep learning and neural networks. The basic idea is there but we need significant amount of data for a good sustainable algorithm. Moreover, the IOT, AI, and ML are some of the most powerful helping tools for this scenario. These powerful assets can develop intelligent predictions and decrease the individual cost along with the foundation cost of natural calamities when they actually occur. Catastrophe modeling is a great initiative which can lead to significant advanced inclusive programs in order to tackle calamities and solve the issues related to them very efficiently [17].

1.9.1 Anticipating Disasters With Better Data

Fortuitously, the technologies and the process of predicting natural calamities keep on evolving into advanced and more accurate systems, allowing national tech bodies like FEMA, NASA, and NOAA to design protocols and solutions. These bodies utilize data from various sources such as satellite pictures, sensors, and so on for predicting natural calamities and find solutions to them in prior. An instance, where natural emergency department can easily look into the situation using AI and cloud-based software which can alarm the towers and the responsible authorities for calamities such as typhoons and tropical disasters. Moreover, data can be collected and used using sensors on trees in case of a draft which is highly common in some parts of the world. One more example is SkyAlert, a high-end functional application which is used in Mexico for the same purpose. It takes advantage of mobiles, computers, and other such devices, and there is one more great example which uses IoT technology on Microsoft’s cloud platform Azure for alarming millions of people before an earthquake or any such thing is about to occur, which ultimately allows the users to escape and move away quickly before the disaster hits [17].

1.9.2 Preparation at A Granular Level

Advanced technologies such as AI and IoT are installed to capture data and real time examples from highways, roads, buildings, and then this data is sent to an organization that takes out meaningful information from the captured data and then uses it to improve the general experience [18]. These operational technologies can make be a great defensive barrier and provide fast instantaneous services to the people during a natural disaster without much panicking and loss of manual labor. When compared to some of the current working models which are based on analog signaling, these AI-based models are an amazing upgrade on the current system and will only continue to grow.

Councils on smaller level which represent a town or a small city can also access AI and IoT to predict natural calamities in their area, and warn the population about a tackle plan which will keep them safe by warning and evacuating the area which is to be affected. During a real life disaster scenario AI and ML could easily come in handy by constantly notifying the masses about safe routes by which they can escape or avoid the damage using relative position of each individual or group. AI can tell which route to take without causing any congestion or confusion if the population is willing to understand its working like WhatsApp. Here is one good example of evacuation using relative positioning. If there is a blast or a natural calamity in stadium or a shopping mall then AI-based models can actually help people find the best exits along with which ones to avoid which will be a huge step forward in this area.

1.9.3 Responding More Efficiently

The three days right after any natural calamity are very crucial. Different set of organizations or concerned bodies should come up with protocols, establish functions to aid, look for any remaining human which can be saved or aided, and initiate programs to make sure that there are no or least possible environmental variations due to the calamity. AI and internet of things (IoT) can massively help in providing in depth insights which can be used for improving disaster traffic, such as [19]:

  • Images from aerial visions and space devices under government;
  • Internet of things and AI base data;
  • Artificial intelligence-powered chatbots;
  • Social media in relation to cloud.

The following ways will assist the responsible authorities to anticipate required aiding materials, plan evacuation protocols, and avoid unimportant steps which will consume time and sources, but only if it’s decipherable. AI is capable of detecting calamities prior to their actual arrival if they are fed with the data of past disaster and with time new data can be generated too so there is only improvements which are going to take place with respect to AI predictive models for natural disasters. Same can be said for ML-based models which can predict any type of disaster with great accuracy [20].

For instance, a big public building is affected by a natural calamity, and they have AI-based help available to them. Using the AI-based the safety team can come with an aid plan for the people stuck or affected which will take the design and the whole body of the affected building in consideration and act accordingly, scan the nearby areas and see how many people require aid which will give the helping team a better idea. It can also figure out the affected transportation assets and help them to show a better way to get out of the red zone area by using google maps or something similar, last but not the least it can help transportation during or after the disaster which will lead to less congestion and a smoother way to keep everyone secure. Using AI and IoT with cloud-based models which uses devices such as mobile can lead to faster traffic management during disasters. In future, when we have enough knowledge and resources, a humanoid based on AI is possible too which will provide fast and save care in congested traffic situations due to calamities [18].

1.9.4 Progressed Planning and Assistance Efforts Through Analytics

AI and ML will be very important in providing assistance to traffic officials as well, it will allow them to help the affected and needy in case of disaster on priority bases and will decrease the threshold time for their assistance, proving to be essential for both of the parties. AI can be implemented to search or predict the most affected (to be) areas by a natural disaster by taking several parameters into account such as season history, disaster history, usual traffic trends and many others by doing this the departments can prepare a plan to help the vehicles in that area and an emergency blueprint as well. These findings and data can also be used to make less prone disaster buildings or highways in areas which are predicted to be affected more, this way natural property damage can be easily avoided [19].

Some disasters are inevitable but most of them can be avoided on road or at least the impact can be minimized, as we have the power of AI to prevent and prejudice the disasters before they becoming a bigger problem, as now the historical data will prove to be essential when dealing with transport problems and we can prepare and manage an evacuation plan for the road traffic, which will lower the number of victims and lower the loss of transport assets [19].

In the times of natural disasters, AI will act as a supervisor and a very responsible one because the potential of AI depends upon the data to some extent and we have enough sources of data to create an ultimate disaster traffic manger based on AI, in near future.

1.10 Traffic Control in Relation To Social Media

At this time, various cities of all over the world are facing lot of problems related to the traffic congestion. The increasing of vehicles, either personal or public transport are the big issue for controlling during the jam. Because of this, the earnings of the countries are reducing. This reason is creating the huge time spent during the traffic jam. However, the huge risk is appeared with traffic jams include vehicle break-ins during the traffic jams.

According to the aforesaid reason, roughly 5 billion unit of electric current and 2 billion gallons of burning products are wasted annually during the traffic jams.

The emergency services like police and medical-related services in the cities, also increasing the vehicles conflicting together by the drivers rashly and avoiding the traffic rules. The congestion of the road is another reason due to increasing the quantity of vehicles. According to this, road is not maintained, transport is not properly planned, and public transport is also not managed, all this creates a huge problem for traffic jams and because of improper traffic management, people are facing big problems for this situation in the cities. In order to manage the traffic jams, the actual time of events occurring on roads would be known to people. There are two types of traffic congestion such as recurrent and non-recurrent.

1.10.1 Recurrent Type of Traffic

This refers to the use of limited space of road every time by a wide range of vehicles.

1.10.2 Non-Recurrent Type of Traffic

This refers to events like weather situations, accidents, vehicles break down all these are random and unplanned and day off celebrations, etc.

According to this, non-recurrent traffic is compounded by the recurrent type of event. Therefore, traffic lights, which is a conventional methods of traffic control are not working on the various roads where applicable. However, the unfavorable effects appeared on the smooth functioning of traffic. As per the situation, the traffic officer should control and manage the traffic so that we can solve the problem during the traffic jam [7]. Now, the Social Network is important in our daily lives. Data is combined along with the picture or video for good explanation in any situation by social media. It provides real time information which is backed by research.

Traffic congestion’s impact should be lessened and policy choices should be informed. If any similar crises occur, it’s copy should be done to fight against that crises. Road traffic administration is influenced both extensively and intensively by social media. Roads that are closed, accidents, and other similar situations on the roads are also accomplished by social media [7].

To manage the huge blockages on road traffic, social media’s opinions and suggestions are utilized. Road jam in cities has triggered the social media criticism, it is being said by literary suggestions. The activation of this discussion is clear that this information is developed and achieved by the network. Individual are also active on multiple groups on various platforms. Hence, Facebook is the most popular platform all over the world for guiding and controlling the road traffic administration. The other social media platform is Twitter, which has played a very important part for controlling and guiding the road traffic management in the cities. According to this, different policies are adopted by different countries which is related to control the road traffic problems. Therefore, small groups are the basic components of social media in which members experience various choices. These non-recurrent traffic leads to not only in one-to-one conversation criticism of authority but also on various networking sites and social media handles [9].

1.11 Autonomous Transportation Vehicle

Automated vehicles is next big thing in transportation that is happening and are in ongoing phase. Automated vehicles testing is happening around the world, even some of them operating on freeways on their own. Arrival of ATVs on roadways is going to impact the way we look at transport and it is going to create a huge turnover within whole transportation [21]. ATVs will create a new era where command of the vehicles will be shifted from user to the AI, which will be the first time where AI takes control of full vehicle and human are just there for the show. Working of ATVs is very simple with help of AI sensors, applications, trackers they will monitor the surrounding and move accordingly. They could even react to sudden movement change by any other cars and can even change course accordingly. AI is playing a huge role in ATVs without AI ATVs could not operate on roads. Many private companies and working on making the first secure ATV which could operate on roads with ease. Many companies are testing their ATVs on freeways, and some are even ready to get to operate on metropolitan streets. If they successfully get their testing done on only, they will take the whole transport system which storm but they could generate uncountable revenue. ATVs not only affect the private sector but it will also create a huge impact on the public sector also. With its coming to the public sector, it could take over not only Road transportation but also airways and seaways also. There is no denying that AI in ATVs will react fast, will be more responsible, more effective, more convenient, and more economical. It can react fast as compared to human senses also [10].

But there are a few concerns that are taking place with ATVs and we don’t have any solution for these concerns. First being what if AI does some mistake and it leads to some serious accident and even damage to property and lives, who will take the blame for this, person who owns the vehicle, company that made that vehicle, AI who was operating that vehicle. As main aim of ATVs is to operate without human interaction and only consult human when necessary, what happen if human could not take over at the given time, which is mostly possible as tested you can’t handle a huge strain in one second when you are resting a second before, it takes some time for human mind to react, so we can’t blame user for anything and even company whose cars were released to operate after full testing and government approval. And in case of AI, you can’t blame or accuse a non-living thing. This concern is huge and as there is no law regarding AI, there is no assurance we are save if something like this happens. So, there should be some rules and regulations that cover AI involvement in ATVs [22].

1.12 Risk and Challenges

No doubt AI can significantly advance efficiency and productivity, but it can also have social and economic consequences that need to be managed. Some of the most significant consequences are mentioned below:

  1. Job Loses: According to a report by the Center for Global Policy Solutions, it is seen that in a transition to AVs in the United States, more than 4 million jobs would likely be lost which include bus drivers, taxi drivers, etc. Ai can accelerate job losses for low-skilled workers in fields like transportation by boosting the development of economic models which are already established as it made a transition toward a service economy.
  2. Regulatory Requirements: When AI systems act wrong just like humans do, then it is hard to foretell what should the regulatory requirements be. It is not easy to understand who would likely be responsible if AV would do any mishap or injury in spite of the fact that according to a research, AVs lessen deaths caused by traffic.
  3. Privacy Concerns: There must be strong privacy laws for ML where users are asked to opt and provide personal data. Too much information on social network should be balanced with the privacy laws.
  4. Lack of Skills and Education: In developed countries, investment in AI is growing and consequently over the last few years AI experts are in demand but the greatest barrier in adoption of AI is scarcity of skilled AI people. AI is a complex technology with social and economic effects which is why it is difficult for a developed country to successfully make use of it. In order to get most of the benefit from AI, it takes time for a developed country to have skilled people and to build effective business strategies.
  5. Cost: In the transport industry, it is seen that AI is not progressing well. The main reason is that AI systems are overpriced. Applications of AI in the transport industry is not fully utilized by inexpensive and not fully developed countries since they are not ready yet to implement AI systems and also to provide services to these systems. To fully utilize the power of AI systems in transportation, countries must be investing in AI and those countries which do not invest in these systems may have difficult time to fully utilize AI power.

1.13 Conclusion

AI and its subset, such as ML and so on, has the potential to exponentially boost the positive output and profit in a lot of public and private sectors, which includes transportation as well, and these changes are not as far as some might anticipate, one can observe and see these changes at this very moment in their daily lives. AI is making transport safer, quicker, and more convenient for normal public right now and it will only continue to grow. For instance, AI-driven drones are delivering medicines and other essentials in places such as Afghanistan and rural Africa, and there are AI-driven traffic management systems that decrease the emission and congestion of traffic and pollution in Japan, and automated cars like tesla and the cargo shuttle in China, some of these do not even need a driver that is how advanced they are. With tremendous ability to increase performance and durability, along with more positives, there are a few negatives which relates to social, demographical, industrial, and political challenges that must be solved in order for peaceful use of the power of AI evolution and progress in every sector of society and universal benefits of the general public, which includes transportation as well [14].
